##### 1. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/buz/spatialDispatch.buzz.ts` *(UPDATE)*

Preserve all existing exports (`dispatchSpatialPayloadsDownstream`, `resolveDefaultEngineRoot`, `buildSpatialChronologyStamp`) in `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/buz/spatialDispatch.buzz.ts`, and add `purgeDownstreamSpatialProofs`:

```typescript
import { assertSafeSpatialResetTarget } from '../../00.spatial.unit/buz/resetGuard.buzz.js'

export interface DownstreamSpatialPurgeResult {
  readonly purgedFiles: number
  readonly purgedSeriesDirs: number
  readonly targetSpatialDir: string
}

export async function purgeDownstreamSpatialProofs(options?: {
  readonly spatialRoot?: string
  readonly engineRoot?: string
  readonly ste?: State
}): Promise<DownstreamSpatialPurgeResult> {
  const spatialRoot = options?.spatialRoot
    ? path.resolve(options.spatialRoot)
    : path.resolve(process.cwd())
  const engineRoot = options?.engineRoot
    ? path.resolve(options.engineRoot)
    : resolveDefaultEngineRoot(spatialRoot)

  const candidateDir = path.join(engineRoot, 'data', 'spatial')
  const safeSpatialDir = assertSafeSpatialResetTarget(
    spatialRoot,
    candidateDir,
    {
      allowDownstreamSibling: true,
      engineRoot,
    },
  )

  let purgedFiles = 0
  let purgedSeriesDirs = 0

  const countFilesRecursively = (dir: string): number => {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return 0
    let count = 0
    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') continue
      const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name)
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        count += countFilesRecursively(fullPath)
      } else if (entry.isFile()) {
        count++
      }
    }
    return count
  }

  if (fs.existsSync(safeSpatialDir)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(safeSpatialDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') continue
      const childPath = path.join(safeSpatialDir, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeSpatialResetTarget(spatialRoot, childPath, {
        allowDownstreamSibling: true,
        engineRoot,
      })
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        purgedFiles += countFilesRecursively(safeChild)
        purgedSeriesDirs++
      } else if (entry.isFile()) {
        purgedFiles++
      }
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
    }
  }

  const line = `>> [SPATIAL:DOWNSTREAM_PURGE] Purged ${purgedFiles} proof file(s) across ${purgedSeriesDirs} engine series folder(s) [OK]`
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await lib
      .hunt('[Console action] Update Console', { idx: 'cns00', src: line })
      .catch(() => {})
  } else if (options?.ste && typeof options.ste.hunt === 'function') {
    await options.ste
      .hunt('[Console action] Update Console', { idx: 'cns00', src: line })
      .catch(() => {})
  }

  return {
    purgedFiles,
    purgedSeriesDirs,
    targetSpatialDir: safeSpatialDir,
  }
}
```

Also re-export `purgeDownstreamSpatialProofs` and `type DownstreamSpatialPurgeResult` in `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/receipt.buzzer.ts`.

##### 2. `packages/301.spatial/00.spatial.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts` *(CREATE)*

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import type { SpatialModel } from '../spatial.model.js'
import type SpatialBit from '../fce/spatial.bit.js'
import {
  SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST,
  type SpatialBlastRadiusInventory,
  type SpatialResetCascadeOrigin,
  type SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt,
  type SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit,
} from '../../38.types.unit/index.js'
import { GENESIS_HASH_64 } from '../../19.ledger.unit/val/ledger.model.js'
import { assertSafeSpatialResetTarget } from './resetGuard.buzz.js'
import {
  purgeDownstreamSpatialProofs,
  resolveDefaultEngineRoot,
} from '../../42.receipt.unit/buz/spatialDispatch.buzz.js'

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'

export const SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1 = 'CONFIRM_1_OF_3'
export const SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2 = 'CONFIRM_2_OF_3'
export const SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3 = 'CONFIRM_3_OF_3'

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim()
}

async function emitSpatialResetTelemetry(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const raw of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(raw)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib
        .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
        .catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste
        .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
        .catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

export function resolveResetSpatialRoot(startDir?: string): string {
  if (startDir && startDir.trim().length > 0) {
    return path.resolve(startDir)
  }
  let curr = path.resolve(process.cwd())
  while (curr && curr !== path.dirname(curr)) {
    if (
      fs.existsSync(path.join(curr, 'packages', '301.spatial')) ||
      fs.existsSync(path.join(curr, '00.spatial.unit'))
    ) {
      return curr
    }
    curr = path.dirname(curr)
  }
  return path.resolve(process.cwd())
}

function countFilesInDir(dir: string, extFilter?: string): number {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return 0
  let total = 0
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') continue
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name)
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      total += countFilesInDir(fullPath, extFilter)
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      if (!extFilter || entry.name.endsWith(extFilter)) {
        total++
      }
    }
  }
  return total
}

function countScratchpadsInDir(dir: string): number {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return 0
  let total = 0
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name)
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      if (
        entry.name === 'node_modules' ||
        entry.name === '.git' ||
        entry.name === 'dist'
      ) {
        continue
      }
      total += countScratchpadsInDir(fullPath)
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      if (
        entry.name.startsWith('.tmp_') ||
        entry.name.includes('.tmp_') ||
        entry.name.endsWith('.tmp')
      ) {
        total++
      }
    }
  }
  return total
}

export async function scanSpatialBlastRadius(
  repoRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
  engineRootInput?: string,
): Promise<SpatialBlastRadiusInventory> {
  const repoRoot = resolveResetSpatialRoot(repoRootInput)
  const engineRoot = engineRootInput
    ? path.resolve(engineRootInput)
    : resolveDefaultEngineRoot(repoRoot)

  const receiptsDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'spatial_receipts')
  const manifestsDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'manifests')
  const localProofsDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'spatial_proofs')
  const stageDraftsDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'drafts', 'spatial_stage')
  const downstreamSpatialDir = path.join(engineRoot, 'data', 'spatial')

  const signedReceiptPackets = countFilesInDir(receiptsDir, '.json')
  const cachedTopologyManifests = countFilesInDir(manifestsDir, '.json')
  const stagedAppointmentProofs =
    countFilesInDir(localProofsDir, '.json') +
    countFilesInDir(stageDraftsDir, '.json')
  const downstreamEngineProofFiles = countFilesInDir(
    downstreamSpatialDir,
    '.json',
  )

  const tempScratchpadFiles =
    countScratchpadsInDir(receiptsDir) +
    countScratchpadsInDir(manifestsDir) +
    countScratchpadsInDir(localProofsDir) +
    countScratchpadsInDir(stageDraftsDir) +
    countScratchpadsInDir(path.join(repoRoot, 'data'))

  const stateVal = (ste as any)?.value || {}
  const loadedHexCells =
    typeof stateVal.topology?.totalLoadedCells === 'number'
      ? stateVal.topology.totalLoadedCells
      : typeof stateVal.database?.topologyDb?.size === 'function'
        ? stateVal.database.topologyDb.size()
        : 0
  const registeredPartitions =
    typeof stateVal.edge?.registeredPartitionCount === 'number'
      ? stateVal.edge.registeredPartitionCount
      : 0
  const ledgerQuadrupleEntries = Array.isArray(stateVal.ledger?.entries)
    ? stateVal.ledger.entries.length
    : 0

  const inventory: SpatialBlastRadiusInventory = {
    cachedTopologyManifests,
    signedReceiptPackets,
    stagedAppointmentProofs,
    downstreamEngineProofFiles,
    loadedHexCells,
    registeredPartitions,
    ledgerQuadrupleEntries,
    tempScratchpadFiles,
  }

  const telemetryLines: readonly string[] = [
    '>> ================================================================',
    '>> [SPATIAL:BLAST_RADIUS] PRE-FLIGHT VOID-STATE INVENTORY SWEEP',
    `>> Cached Manifests  : ${inventory.cachedTopologyManifests} topology manifest(s)`,
    `>> Signed Receipts   : ${inventory.signedReceiptPackets} receipt packet(s)`,
    `>> Staged Proofs     : ${inventory.stagedAppointmentProofs} local | ${inventory.downstreamEngineProofFiles} engine handoff`,
    `>> Scratchpad Files  : ${inventory.tempScratchpadFiles} temporary buffer(s)`,
    `>> Live Heap State   : Cells=${inventory.loadedHexCells} | Partitions=${inventory.registeredPartitions} | LedgerEntries=${inventory.ledgerQuadrupleEntries}`,
    '>> ================================================================',
  ]
  await emitSpatialResetTelemetry(ste, telemetryLines)

  return inventory
}

function purgeDirectoryContentsSafe(
  repoRoot: string,
  targetDir: string,
  ensureContainer: boolean,
): number {
  const safeDir = assertSafeSpatialResetTarget(repoRoot, targetDir)
  let removedFiles = 0

  if (fs.existsSync(safeDir)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(safeDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') continue
      const childPath = path.join(safeDir, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeSpatialResetTarget(repoRoot, childPath)
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        removedFiles += countFilesInDir(safeChild)
      } else if (entry.isFile()) {
        removedFiles++
      }
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
    }
  } else if (ensureContainer) {
    fs.mkdirSync(safeDir, { recursive: true })
  }

  return removedFiles
}

function purgeOrphanedScratchpadsSafe(repoRoot: string): number {
  const scanRoots = [
    path.join(repoRoot, 'spatial_receipts'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'drafts'),
  ]
  let removed = 0

  const sweep = (currentDir: string): void => {
    if (!fs.existsSync(currentDir)) return
    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(currentDir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      const fullPath = path.join(currentDir, entry.name)
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        if (entry.name === 'directive' || entry.name === 'overview' || entry.name === 'building') {
          continue
        }
        sweep(fullPath)
      } else if (entry.isFile()) {
        if (
          entry.name.startsWith('.tmp_') ||
          entry.name.includes('.tmp_') ||
          entry.name.endsWith('.tmp')
        ) {
          const safeFile = assertSafeSpatialResetTarget(repoRoot, fullPath)
          fs.rmSync(safeFile, { force: true })
          removed++
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (const root of scanRoots) {
    sweep(root)
  }
  return removed
}

export interface SpatialVoidVerificationResult {
  readonly isVoidState: boolean
  readonly residualDiskArtifacts: number
  readonly ledgerHeadHash: string
  readonly loadedManifestCount: number
}

export async function verifySpatialVoidState(
  repoRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
  engineRootInput?: string,
): Promise<SpatialVoidVerificationResult> {
  const postInventory = await scanSpatialBlastRadius(
    repoRootInput,
    undefined,
    engineRootInput,
  )
  const residualDiskArtifacts =
    postInventory.cachedTopologyManifests +
    postInventory.signedReceiptPackets +
    postInventory.stagedAppointmentProofs +
    postInventory.downstreamEngineProofFiles +
    postInventory.tempScratchpadFiles

  const stateVal = (ste as any)?.value || {}
  const ledgerHeadHash =
    typeof stateVal.ledger?.lastEventHash === 'string'
      ? stateVal.ledger.lastEventHash
      : GENESIS_HASH_64
  const loadedManifestCount =
    typeof stateVal.database?.topologyDb?.size === 'function'
      ? stateVal.database.topologyDb.size()
      : 0

  if (stateVal.menu && typeof stateVal.menu === 'object') {
    stateVal.menu.statusMessage = 'SPATIAL_VOID_STATE_READY'
    stateVal.menu.lastAuditSummary = null
  }

  const isVoidState =
    residualDiskArtifacts === 0 &&
    loadedManifestCount === 0 &&
    ledgerHeadHash === GENESIS_HASH_64

  await emitSpatialResetTelemetry(ste, [
    `>> [SPATIAL:VOID_VERIFIED] isVoidState=${isVoidState} | ResidualArtifacts=${residualDiskArtifacts} | LedgerHead=${ledgerHeadHash.slice(0, 12)}... [OK]`,
  ])

  return {
    isVoidState,
    residualDiskArtifacts,
    ledgerHeadHash,
    loadedManifestCount,
  }
}

export const executeTabulaRasaSpatial = async (
  cpy: SpatialModel,
  bal?: SpatialBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<SpatialModel> => {
  const datObj = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const req: SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (datObj.tabulaRasaRequest as SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit) || datObj

  const repoRoot = resolveResetSpatialRoot(
    req.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const engineRoot = req.engineRoot
    ? path.resolve(req.engineRoot)
    : resolveDefaultEngineRoot(repoRoot)
  const cascadeOrigin: SpatialResetCascadeOrigin =
    req.cascadeOrigin || 'LOCAL_TUI'

  const zeroInventory: SpatialBlastRadiusInventory = {
    cachedTopologyManifests: 0,
    signedReceiptPackets: 0,
    stagedAppointmentProofs: 0,
    downstreamEngineProofFiles: 0,
    loadedHexCells: 0,
    registeredPartitions: 0,
    ledgerQuadrupleEntries: 0,
    tempScratchpadFiles: 0,
  }

  let abortStage: 1 | 2 | 3 | undefined
  if (req.confirmToken1 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1) {
    abortStage = 1
  } else if (req.confirmToken2 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2) {
    abortStage = 2
  } else if (req.confirmToken3 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3) {
    abortStage = 3
  }

  if (abortStage !== undefined) {
    const abortLine = `:: [SPATIAL:TABULA_RASA_ABORTED] Triple-Lock verification failed at Stage ${abortStage}/3. Workspace untouched.`
    await emitSpatialResetTelemetry(ste, [abortLine])

    const abortedReceipt: SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt = {
      ok: false,
      voidStateVerified: false,
      cascadeOrigin,
      purgedDiskCounts: zeroInventory,
      resetUnitIds: [],
      genesisLedgerHeadHash: GENESIS_HASH_64,
      timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      receipts: [abortLine],
    }

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        sptBit: {
          idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-spatial-aborted',
          val: 0,
          dat: abortedReceipt,
        } as any,
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  const preInventory = await scanSpatialBlastRadius(repoRoot, ste, engineRoot)
  const receipts: string[] = [
    `>> [SPATIAL:PURGE_DISK] Initiating Tabula Rasa purge (origin=${cascadeOrigin})...`,
  ]

  const purgedReceipts = purgeDirectoryContentsSafe(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'spatial_receipts'),
    true,
  )
  const purgedManifests = purgeDirectoryContentsSafe(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'manifests'),
    true,
  )
  const purgedLocalProofs = purgeDirectoryContentsSafe(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'spatial_proofs'),
    true,
  )
  const purgedStageDrafts = purgeDirectoryContentsSafe(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'drafts', 'spatial_stage'),
    false,
  )
  const purgedScratchpads = purgeOrphanedScratchpadsSafe(repoRoot)

  const downstreamPurge = await purgeDownstreamSpatialProofs({
    spatialRoot: repoRoot,
    engineRoot,
    ste,
  })

  receipts.push(
    `>> [SPATIAL:PURGE_DISK] Receipts=${purgedReceipts} | Manifests=${purgedManifests} | LocalProofs=${purgedLocalProofs + purgedStageDrafts} | Scratchpads=${purgedScratchpads} [OK]`,
  )

  // Zero local SpatialModel counters
  cpy.totalSynchronousInvocations = 0
  cpy.totalBudgetsTripped = 0
  cpy.lastExecutionMicros = 0

  // Dispatch Phase 2 RESET_<UNIT> actions across all 16 STATEFUL_RESETTABLE units
  const resetUnitIds: string[] = []
  for (const unitDesc of SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST.unitRegistry) {
    if (
      unitDesc.classification === 'STATEFUL_RESETTABLE' &&
      unitDesc.resetActionConstant
    ) {
      resetUnitIds.push(unitDesc.unitFolder)
      if (
        unitDesc.unitFolder !== '00.spatial.unit' &&
        ste &&
        typeof ste.hunt === 'function'
      ) {
        await ste.hunt(unitDesc.resetActionConstant, {}).catch(() => {})
      }
    }
  }

  receipts.push(
    `>> [SPATIAL:ZERO_UNITS] Re-initialized ${resetUnitIds.length} stateful spatial unit(s) -> VOID STATE [OK]`,
  )

  const voidCheck = await verifySpatialVoidState(repoRoot, ste, engineRoot)
  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:COMPLETE] 000.goblin-spatial restored to Byte-0 Void State [PASS]`,
  )

  await emitSpatialResetTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  const finalReceipt: SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt = {
    ok: voidCheck.isVoidState,
    voidStateVerified: voidCheck.isVoidState,
    cascadeOrigin,
    purgedDiskCounts: {
      cachedTopologyManifests: purgedManifests,
      signedReceiptPackets: purgedReceipts,
      stagedAppointmentProofs: purgedLocalProofs + purgedStageDrafts,
      downstreamEngineProofFiles: downstreamPurge.purgedFiles,
      loadedHexCells: preInventory.loadedHexCells,
      registeredPartitions: preInventory.registeredPartitions,
      ledgerQuadrupleEntries: preInventory.ledgerQuadrupleEntries,
      tempScratchpadFiles: purgedScratchpads,
    },
    resetUnitIds,
    genesisLedgerHeadHash: voidCheck.ledgerHeadHash,
    timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
    receipts,
  }

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      sptBit: {
        idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-spatial-pass',
        val: voidCheck.isVoidState ? 1 : 0,
        dat: finalReceipt,
      } as any,
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

#### III. CONCRETE FILE IMPLEMENTATIONS

##### 1. `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.ts` *(CREATE)*

```typescript
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js'
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js'
import {
  executeTabulaRasaSpatial,
  resolveResetSpatialRoot,
  scanSpatialBlastRadius,
  SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
} from '../../00.spatial.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.js'
import { SpatialModel } from '../../00.spatial.unit/spatial.model.js'
import { GENESIS_HASH_64 } from '../../19.ledger.unit/val/ledger.model.js'
import type {
  SpatialResetCascadeOrigin,
  SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt,
  SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit,
} from '../../38.types.unit/index.js'

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid'
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice'
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'

export const TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_1_CHOICES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  '[NO] CANCEL & PRESERVE SPATIAL STATE',
  '[CONFIRM 1/3] INITIATE SPATIAL TABULA RASA RESET',
])

export const TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_2_CHOICES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  '[NO] ABORT SPATIAL RESET IMMEDIATELY',
  '[NO] RETAIN TOPOLOGY CACHE & RECEIPTS',
  '[CONFIRM 2/3] ACKNOWLEDGE OBLITERATION OF SPATIAL PROOFS & CACHES',
])

export const TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_3_CHOICES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  '[NO] STAND DOWN & DISARM SPATIAL RESET',
  '[CONFIRM 3/3] EXECUTE GRAND BEGINNING (SPATIAL BYTE-0 VOID STATE)',
])

export interface SpatialTripleLockGateOutcome {
  readonly cleared: boolean
  readonly abortedAtStage?: 1 | 2 | 3
  readonly abortReason?: string
  readonly tokens?: {
    readonly confirmToken1: typeof SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1
    readonly confirmToken2: typeof SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2
    readonly confirmToken3: typeof SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  }
}

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim()
}

function resolveLibraryBus(): any {
  return (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY || null
}

async function emitConsoleLine(
  ste: State | undefined,
  line: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const clean = sanitizeAscii(line)
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus()
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await lib
      .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
      .catch(() => {})
  } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    await ste
      .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
      .catch(() => {})
  }
}

async function promptRedAlertChoice(
  lib: any,
  choices: readonly string[],
): Promise<{ src: string; val: number }> {
  const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
    x: 0,
    y: 4,
    xSpan: 4,
    ySpan: 8,
  })

  const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
    dat: { clr0: 'black', clr1: 'red' },
    src: 'vertical',
    lst: [...choices],
    net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
  })

  const src = String(choiceBit?.chcBit?.src || '')
  const val =
    typeof choiceBit?.chcBit?.val === 'number' ? choiceBit.chcBit.val : -1
  return { src, val }
}

export function verifySpatialHeadlessTripleLockTokens(
  payload: Record<string, any> | undefined,
): SpatialTripleLockGateOutcome {
  const dat = payload || {}
  const req =
    (dat.tabulaRasaRequest as Record<string, any> | undefined) || dat

  if (req.confirmToken1 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1) {
    return {
      cleared: false,
      abortedAtStage: 1,
      abortReason: 'Missing or invalid confirmToken1 (expected CONFIRM_1_OF_3)',
    }
  }
  if (req.confirmToken2 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2) {
    return {
      cleared: false,
      abortedAtStage: 2,
      abortReason: 'Missing or invalid confirmToken2 (expected CONFIRM_2_OF_3)',
    }
  }
  if (req.confirmToken3 !== SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3) {
    return {
      cleared: false,
      abortedAtStage: 3,
      abortReason: 'Missing or invalid confirmToken3 (expected CONFIRM_3_OF_3)',
    }
  }

  return {
    cleared: true,
    tokens: {
      confirmToken1: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
      confirmToken2: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
      confirmToken3: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
    },
  }
}

export async function runSpatialTripleLockStageOne(
  lib: any,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<boolean> {
  await emitConsoleLine(
    ste,
    '>> [RESET:STAGE 1/3] TRIPLE-LOCK INTERLOCK ENGAGED (Default Index 0 = [NO])',
  )
  const res = await promptRedAlertChoice(lib, TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_1_CHOICES)
  const confirmed =
    res.val === 1 || res.src === TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_1_CHOICES[1]

  if (!confirmed) {
    await emitConsoleLine(
      ste,
      '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 1/3. System untouched.',
    )
    return false
  }
  return true
}

export async function runSpatialTripleLockStageTwo(
  lib: any,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<boolean> {
  await emitConsoleLine(
    ste,
    '>> [RESET:LOCK_1_CLEARED] Stage 1/3 verified. Awaiting Stage 2/3 confirmation...',
  )
  const res = await promptRedAlertChoice(lib, TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_2_CHOICES)
  const confirmed =
    res.val === 2 || res.src === TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_2_CHOICES[2]

  if (!confirmed) {
    await emitConsoleLine(
      ste,
      '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 2/3. System untouched.',
    )
    return false
  }
  return true
}

export async function runSpatialTripleLockStageThree(
  lib: any,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<boolean> {
  await emitConsoleLine(
    ste,
    '>> [RESET:LOCK_2_CLEARED] Stage 2/3 verified. ARMED FOR FINAL EXECUTION (Stage 3/3)...',
  )
  const res = await promptRedAlertChoice(lib, TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_3_CHOICES)
  const confirmed =
    res.val === 1 || res.src === TRIPLE_LOCK_STAGE_3_CHOICES[1]

  if (!confirmed) {
    await emitConsoleLine(
      ste,
      '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 3/3. System untouched.',
    )
    return false
  }
  return true
}

export const runSpatialTabulaRasaMenu = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal?: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const datObj = ((bal as any)?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const reqObj: SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (datObj.tabulaRasaRequest as SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit) || datObj

  const repoRoot = resolveResetSpatialRoot(
    reqObj.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const engineRoot = reqObj.engineRoot
    ? path.resolve(reqObj.engineRoot)
    : undefined
  const cascadeOrigin: SpatialResetCascadeOrigin =
    reqObj.cascadeOrigin || 'LOCAL_TUI'

  const lib = resolveLibraryBus()
  let gateOutcome: SpatialTripleLockGateOutcome

  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await scanSpatialBlastRadius(repoRoot, ste, engineRoot)

    const pass1 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageOne(lib, ste)
    if (!pass1) {
      gateOutcome = {
        cleared: false,
        abortedAtStage: 1,
        abortReason: 'Operator declined Stage 1/3 confirmation',
      }
    } else {
      const pass2 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageTwo(lib, ste)
      if (!pass2) {
        gateOutcome = {
          cleared: false,
          abortedAtStage: 2,
          abortReason: 'Operator declined Stage 2/3 confirmation',
        }
      } else {
        const pass3 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageThree(lib, ste)
        if (!pass3) {
          gateOutcome = {
            cleared: false,
            abortedAtStage: 3,
            abortReason: 'Operator declined Stage 3/3 confirmation',
          }
        } else {
          gateOutcome = {
            cleared: true,
            tokens: {
              confirmToken1: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
              confirmToken2: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
              confirmToken3: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
            },
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    gateOutcome = verifySpatialHeadlessTripleLockTokens(datObj)
    if (!gateOutcome.cleared) {
      await emitConsoleLine(
        ste,
        `>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage ${gateOutcome.abortedAtStage}/3. System untouched.`,
      )
    }
  }

  if (!gateOutcome.cleared || !gateOutcome.tokens) {
    cpy.statusMessage = `SPATIAL_RESET_ABORTED_STAGE_${gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1}`
    const abortReceipt: SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt = {
      ok: false,
      voidStateVerified: false,
      cascadeOrigin,
      purgedDiskCounts: {
        cachedTopologyManifests: 0,
        signedReceiptPackets: 0,
        stagedAppointmentProofs: 0,
        downstreamEngineProofFiles: 0,
        loadedHexCells: 0,
        registeredPartitions: 0,
        ledgerQuadrupleEntries: 0,
        tempScratchpadFiles: 0,
      },
      resetUnitIds: [],
      genesisLedgerHeadHash: GENESIS_HASH_64,
      timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      receipts: [
        `>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage ${gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1}/3. System untouched.`,
      ],
    }

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'spatial-tabula-rasa-aborted',
          val: 0,
          src: 'ABORTED',
          dat: {
            abortedAtStage: gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1,
            abortReason: gateOutcome.abortReason,
            receipt: abortReceipt,
          },
        } as any,
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  let purgeReceipt: SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt | undefined
  const spatialModel =
    ((ste as any)?.value?.spatial as SpatialModel) || new SpatialModel()

  await executeTabulaRasaSpatial(
    spatialModel,
    {
      idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-spatial',
      src: repoRoot,
      dat: {
        repoRoot,
        engineRoot,
        cascadeOrigin,
        confirmToken1: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken1,
        confirmToken2: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken2,
        confirmToken3: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken3,
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        purgeReceipt = res?.sptBit?.dat as SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt
      },
    } as any,
    ste,
  )

  cpy.statusMessage = 'SPATIAL_VOID_STATE_READY'
  cpy.lastAuditSummary = null

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'spatial-tabula-rasa-pass',
        val: 1,
        src: 'SPATIAL_VOID_STATE_READY',
        dat: purgeReceipt,
      } as any,
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

##### 3. `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.ts` *(UPDATE)*

Append `resolveFleetWorkspaceRoot`, `streamSpatialResetProgressFrames`, and `triggerCauldronFleetCascadeBuzz` to `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.ts`:

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs'

export const REVERSE_CAUSAL_FLEET_REPOS: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  '000.series-sower-conductor',
  '000.series-sower-engine',
  '000.goblin-spatial',
  '000.goblin-lore',
])

export function resolveFleetWorkspaceRoot(startDir?: string): string {
  let curr = path.resolve(startDir || process.cwd())
  while (curr && curr !== path.dirname(curr)) {
    const hasCauldron = fs.existsSync(
      path.join(curr, '000.camp-candor-cauldron'),
    )
    const hasSpatial = fs.existsSync(path.join(curr, '000.goblin-spatial'))
    const hasEngine = fs.existsSync(path.join(curr, '000.series-sower-engine'))
    if (hasCauldron || (hasSpatial && hasEngine)) {
      return curr
    }
    curr = path.dirname(curr)
  }
  return path.resolve(startDir || process.cwd(), '..')
}

export async function streamSpatialResetProgressFrames(
  ste: State | undefined,
  frames: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  for (const frame of frames) {
    await emitConsoleLine(ste, frame)
    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve))
  }
}

export const triggerCauldronFleetCascadeBuzz = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal?: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const datObj = ((bal as any)?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const reqObj: SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (datObj.tabulaRasaRequest as SpatialTabulaRasaRequestBit) || datObj

  const spatialRoot = resolveResetSpatialRoot(
    reqObj.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const fleetRoot = datObj.fleetRoot
    ? path.resolve(datObj.fleetRoot)
    : resolveFleetWorkspaceRoot(spatialRoot)

  cpy.activeMenuMode = 'TABULA_RASA_RESET'
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus()

  await streamSpatialResetProgressFrames(ste, [
    '>> ================================================================',
    '>> [CAULDRON:MASTER_CASCADE] 4-REPOSITORY TABULA RASA RESET ARMED',
    `>> Fleet Root : ${fleetRoot}`,
    '>> Drain Order: 1.Conductor -> 2.Engine -> 3.Spatial -> 4.Lore',
    '>> ================================================================',
  ])

  let gateOutcome: SpatialTripleLockGateOutcome
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await scanSpatialBlastRadius(
      spatialRoot,
      ste,
      path.join(fleetRoot, '000.series-sower-engine'),
    )
    const pass1 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageOne(lib, ste)
    if (!pass1) {
      gateOutcome = {
        cleared: false,
        abortedAtStage: 1,
        abortReason: 'Operator cancelled Master Cauldron Cascade at Stage 1/3',
      }
    } else {
      const pass2 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageTwo(lib, ste)
      if (!pass2) {
        gateOutcome = {
          cleared: false,
          abortedAtStage: 2,
          abortReason: 'Operator cancelled Master Cauldron Cascade at Stage 2/3',
        }
      } else {
        const pass3 = await runSpatialTripleLockStageThree(lib, ste)
        if (!pass3) {
          gateOutcome = {
            cleared: false,
            abortedAtStage: 3,
            abortReason: 'Operator cancelled Master Cauldron Cascade at Stage 3/3',
          }
        } else {
          gateOutcome = {
            cleared: true,
            tokens: {
              confirmToken1: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
              confirmToken2: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
              confirmToken3: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
            },
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    gateOutcome = verifySpatialHeadlessTripleLockTokens(datObj)
  }

  if (!gateOutcome.cleared || !gateOutcome.tokens) {
    cpy.activeMenuMode = 'ROOT'
    cpy.statusMessage = `CAULDRON_CASCADE_ABORTED_STAGE_${gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1}`
    await emitConsoleLine(
      ste,
      `>> [CAULDRON:ABORTED] Master Fleet Cascade cancelled at Stage ${gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1}/3. Fleet untouched.`,
    )
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'cauldron-fleet-cascade-aborted',
          val: 0,
          src: 'ABORTED',
          dat: {
            abortedAtStage: gateOutcome.abortedAtStage ?? 1,
            abortReason: gateOutcome.abortReason,
          },
        } as any,
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await lib
      .hunt('[Cauldron action] Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset', {
        src: fleetRoot,
        dat: {
          fleetRoot,
          confirmToken1: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken1,
          confirmToken2: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken2,
          confirmToken3: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken3,
        },
      })
      .catch(() => {})
  }

  const cascadeReceipts: string[] = []
  let spatialStepReceipt: SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt | undefined

  for (let i = 0; i < REVERSE_CAUSAL_FLEET_REPOS.length; i++) {
    const repoName = REVERSE_CAUSAL_FLEET_REPOS[i]
    const stepNum = i + 1
    const targetRepoPath = path.join(fleetRoot, repoName)
    const repoExists = fs.existsSync(targetRepoPath)

    if (repoName === '000.goblin-spatial') {
      const spatialModel =
        ((ste as any)?.value?.spatial as SpatialModel) || new SpatialModel()
      await executeTabulaRasaSpatial(
        spatialModel,
        {
          idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-spatial',
          src: spatialRoot,
          dat: {
            repoRoot: spatialRoot,
            engineRoot: path.join(fleetRoot, '000.series-sower-engine'),
            cascadeOrigin: 'CAULDRON_MASTER_CASCADE',
            confirmToken1: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken1,
            confirmToken2: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken2,
            confirmToken3: gateOutcome.tokens.confirmToken3,
          },
          slv: (res: any) => {
            spatialStepReceipt = res?.sptBit?.dat as SpatialTabulaRasaReceipt
          },
        } as any,
        ste,
      )
      const line = `>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE ${stepNum}/4] ${repoName} -> VOID STATE VERIFIED [OK]`
      cascadeReceipts.push(line)
      await streamSpatialResetProgressFrames(ste, [line])
    } else {
      const statusTag = repoExists ? 'DISPATCHED [OK]' : 'SIBLING_ABSENT [SKIP]'
      const line = `>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE ${stepNum}/4] ${repoName} -> ${statusTag}`
      cascadeReceipts.push(line)
      await streamSpatialResetProgressFrames(ste, [line])
    }
  }

  const summaryLine =
    '>> [CAULDRON:MASTER_CASCADE] All 4 fleet repositories drained to Byte-0 Void State [PASS]'
  cascadeReceipts.push(summaryLine)
  await streamSpatialResetProgressFrames(ste, [summaryLine])

  cpy.activeMenuMode = 'ROOT'
  cpy.statusMessage = 'SPATIAL_VOID_STATE_READY'
  cpy.lastAuditSummary = null

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'cauldron-fleet-cascade-pass',
        val: 1,
        src: 'CAULDRON_CASCADE_COMPLETE',
        lst: cascadeReceipts,
        dat: {
          fleetRoot,
          sequence: REVERSE_CAUSAL_FLEET_REPOS,
          spatialReceipt: spatialStepReceipt,
          receipts: cascadeReceipts,
        },
      } as any,
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

```typescript
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'

export interface SpatialResetCoverageReport {
  readonly valid: boolean
  readonly packageRoot: string
  readonly mountedUnitCount: number
  readonly statefulUnitCount: number
  readonly statelessUnitCount: number
  readonly unhookedUnits: readonly string[]
  readonly missingWiringDiagnostics: readonly string[]
  readonly receipts: readonly string[]
}

export interface SpatialVoidStateParityReport {
  readonly valid: boolean
  readonly sandboxRoot: string
  readonly initialVoidVerified: boolean
  readonly mutatedActiveVerified: boolean
  readonly postResetVoidVerified: boolean
  readonly zeroOrphanFilesVerified: boolean
  readonly zeroModelContaminationVerified: boolean
  readonly errors: readonly string[]
  readonly receipts: readonly string[]
}

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

async function emitSentinelTelemetry(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const raw of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(raw)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib
        .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
        .catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste
        .hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean })
        .catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

export function resolveSpatialPackageDirectory(
  startDir: string = process.cwd(),
): string {
  const resolvedStart = path.resolve(startDir)
  if (
    fs.existsSync(path.join(resolvedStart, 'BEE.ts')) &&
    fs.existsSync(path.join(resolvedStart, '00.spatial.unit'))
  ) {
    return resolvedStart
  }
  const candidate = path.join(resolvedStart, 'packages', '301.spatial')
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(candidate, 'BEE.ts'))) {
    return candidate
  }
  let curr = resolvedStart
  while (curr && curr !== path.dirname(curr)) {
    const nested = path.join(curr, 'packages', '301.spatial')
    if (fs.existsSync(path.join(nested, 'BEE.ts'))) {
      return nested
    }
    curr = path.dirname(curr)
  }
  return candidate
}

/**
 * Task 6.1: Pure BEE unit static self-verification sentinel auditing BEE.ts
 * unit registrations and stateful RESET_<UNIT> action/reducer wiring against
 * SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST.
 */
export async function auditSpatialResetCoverage(
  packageRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<SpatialResetCoverageReport> {
  const packageRoot = resolveSpatialPackageDirectory(
    packageRootInput || process.cwd(),
  )
  const beePath = path.join(packageRoot, 'BEE.ts')
  const unhookedUnits: string[] = []
  const missingWiringDiagnostics: string[] = []

  const manifest = validateSpatialResetManifest(SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST)

  let mountedUnits: string[] = []
  if (fs.existsSync(beePath)) {
    const beeSrc = fs.readFileSync(beePath, 'utf8')
    const matches = [
      ...beeSrc.matchAll(/\.\/(\d{2}\.[a-z0-9-]+\.unit)\//g),
    ].map((m) => m[1])
    mountedUnits = [...new Set(matches)].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
  } else {
    missingWiringDiagnostics.push(`Missing BEE.ts manifest at ${beePath}`)
  }

  const registryMap = new Map(
    manifest.unitRegistry.map((entry) => [entry.unitFolder, entry]),
  )

  for (const unitFolder of mountedUnits) {
    const descriptor = registryMap.get(unitFolder)
    if (!descriptor) {
      unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
      missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
        `Unregistered unit in BEE.ts: ${unitFolder} missing from SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST.unitRegistry`,
      )
      continue
    }

    if (descriptor.classification === 'STATEFUL_RESETTABLE') {
      const unitSlug = unitFolder.replace(/^\d{2}\./, '').replace(/\.unit$/, '')
      const actionPath = path.join(
        packageRoot,
        unitFolder,
        `${unitSlug}.action.ts`,
      )
      const reducePath = path.join(
        packageRoot,
        unitFolder,
        `${unitSlug}.reduce.ts`,
      )
      const expectedConstant = `RESET_${unitSlug.toUpperCase().replace(/-/g, '_')}`

      if (fs.existsSync(actionPath)) {
        const actionSrc = fs.readFileSync(actionPath, 'utf8')
        if (
          !actionSrc.includes(expectedConstant) &&
          descriptor.resetActionConstant &&
          !actionSrc.includes(descriptor.resetActionConstant)
        ) {
          unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
          missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
            `Stateful unit ${unitFolder} missing ${expectedConstant} in ${unitSlug}.action.ts`,
          )
        }
      }

      if (fs.existsSync(reducePath)) {
        const reduceSrc = fs.readFileSync(reducePath, 'utf8')
        if (!reduceSrc.includes(`Act.${expectedConstant}`)) {
          unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
          missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
            `Stateful unit ${unitFolder} missing Act.${expectedConstant} case in ${unitSlug}.reduce.ts`,
          )
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const statefulUnitCount = manifest.unitRegistry.filter(
    (u) => u.classification === 'STATEFUL_RESETTABLE',
  ).length
  const statelessUnitCount = manifest.unitRegistry.filter(
    (u) => u.classification === 'STATELESS_PURE',
  ).length

  const uniqueUnhooked = [...new Set(unhookedUnits)]
  const valid =
    mountedUnits.length > 0 &&
    uniqueUnhooked.length === 0 &&
    missingWiringDiagnostics.length === 0

  const receipts: string[] = [
    '>> ============================================================',
    '>> [SENTINEL:COVERAGE] SPATIAL TABULA RASA RESET COVERAGE AUDIT',
    `>> Package Root     : ${packageRoot}`,
    `>> Mounted Units    : ${mountedUnits.length} BEE.ts units scanned`,
    `>> Registry Split   : ${statefulUnitCount} stateful | ${statelessUnitCount} stateless`,
    `>> Unhooked Units   : ${uniqueUnhooked.length === 0 ? 'NONE [OK]' : uniqueUnhooked.join(', ')}`,
    `>> Sentinel Verdict : ${valid ? 'PASS [OK]' : 'FAIL [BREACH]'}`,
    '>> ============================================================',
  ]

  await emitSentinelTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  return {
    valid,
    packageRoot,
    mountedUnitCount: mountedUnits.length,
    statefulUnitCount,
    statelessUnitCount,
    unhookedUnits: uniqueUnhooked,
    missingWiringDiagnostics,
    receipts,
  }
}

/**
 * Task 6.2: Executes an isolated 4-step round-trip Void-State parity proof
 * (Void_0 -> Mutated Spatial State -> Triple-Lock Reset -> Void_0 Parity).
 */
export async function verifySpatialRoundTripVoidParity(
  sandboxRoot: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<SpatialVoidStateParityReport> {
  const errors: string[] = []
  const resolvedSandbox = path.resolve(sandboxRoot)
  const spatialRoot = path.join(resolvedSandbox, '000.goblin-spatial')
  const engineRoot = path.join(resolvedSandbox, '000.series-sower-engine')

  fs.mkdirSync(spatialRoot, { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(engineRoot, { recursive: true })

  const spatialModel = new SpatialModel()
  const dbModel = new DatabaseModel()
  const topologyModel = new TopologyModel()
  const ledgerModel = new LedgerModel()
  const occupancyModel = new OccupancyModel()
  const edgeModel = new EdgeModel()
  const capacityModel = new CapacityModel()
  const overlayModel = new OverlayModel()
  const clockModel = new ClockModel()
  const incidentModel = new IncidentModel()
  const witnessModel = new WitnessModel()
  const receiptModel = new ReceiptModel()
  const portModel = new PortModel()
  const collectModel = new CollectModel()
  const menuModel = new MenuModel()

  const localBus: any = {
    value: {
      spatial: spatialModel,
      database: dbModel,
      topology: topologyModel,
      ledger: ledgerModel,
      occupancy: occupancyModel,
      edge: edgeModel,
      capacity: capacityModel,
      overlay: overlayModel,
      clock: clockModel,
      incident: incidentModel,
      witness: witnessModel,
      receipt: receiptModel,
      port: portModel,
      collect: collectModel,
      menu: menuModel,
    },
    hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
      switch (type) {
        case '[Database action] Reset Database':
          await resetDatabase(dbModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Topology action] Reset Topology':
          await resetTopology(topologyModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Ledger action] Reset Ledger':
          await resetLedger(ledgerModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Occupancy action] Reset Occupancy':
          await resetOccupancy(occupancyModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Edge action] Reset Edge':
          await resetEdge(edgeModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Capacity action] Reset Capacity':
          await resetCapacity(capacityModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Overlay action] Reset Overlay':
          await resetOverlay(overlayModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Clock action] Reset Clock':
          await resetClock(clockModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Incident action] Reset Incident':
          await resetIncident(incidentModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Witness action] Reset Witness':
          await resetWitness(witnessModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Receipt action] Reset Receipt':
          await resetReceipt(receiptModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Port action] Reset Port':
          await resetPort(portModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Collect action] Reset Collect':
          await resetCollect(collectModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Menu action] Reset Menu':
          await resetMenu(menuModel, bale, localBus)
          break
      }
      return {}
    },
  }

  // Step 1: Verify initial Void_0 baseline
  const initialVoid = await verifySpatialVoidState(
    spatialRoot,
    localBus,
    engineRoot,
  )
  const initialVoidVerified =
    initialVoid.isVoidState && initialVoid.residualDiskArtifacts === 0
  if (!initialVoidVerified) {
    errors.push('Sandbox did not start in clean Spatial Void State')
  }

  // Step 2: Mutate disk directories and all stateful models
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(spatialRoot, 'spatial_receipts'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(spatialRoot, 'data', 'manifests'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(engineRoot, 'data', 'spatial', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), {
    recursive: true,
  })
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(spatialRoot, 'spatial_receipts', 'rcp_gotham_01.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ ok: true }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(spatialRoot, 'data', 'manifests', 'gotham-topology.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ topologyId: 'loc_gotham_rooftop' }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(
      engineRoot,
      'data',
      'spatial',
      'gotham-trinity-crisis',
      'spatial_proof_001.json',
    ),
    JSON.stringify({ ok: true }),
    'utf8',
  )

  spatialModel.totalSynchronousInvocations = 42
  dbModel.loadedManifestCount = 1
  dbModel.activeRootHash = 'a'.repeat(64)
  topologyModel.totalLoadedCells = 91
  topologyModel.lastCompiledRootHash = 'a'.repeat(64)
  appendEntry(ledgerModel, {
    idx: 'append-test',
    dat: {
      queryType: 'RAYCAST',
      tickNumber: 1,
      auditQuadruple: {
        topologyHash: 'a'.repeat(64),
        overlayHash: 'b'.repeat(64),
        queryHash: 'c'.repeat(64),
        receiptHash: 'd'.repeat(64),
      },
    },
  })
  occupancyModel.occupiedCells.set(2147516416, ['char_wonder_woman'])
  occupancyModel.totalTrackedActors = 1
  edgeModel.partitionDampingOverrides.set(1n, 2200)
  capacityModel.cellCapacityOverrides.set(2147516416, 4)
  overlayModel.closedPortalIds.add('portal_rooftop_hatch')
  ;(globalThis as any).ACTIVE_SPATIAL_CLOCK_SNAPSHOT = { daypart: 'NIGHT' }
  incidentModel.incidentReceiptCount = 2
  witnessModel.stagedAppointmentProofs = [{ id: 'apt_01' }]
  receiptModel.mintedReceipts.set('rcp_01', { ok: true })
  receiptModel.totalReceiptsMinted = 1
  portModel.totalQueriesProcessed = 1000
  collectModel.totalBatchesProcessed = 5
  menuModel.statusMessage = 'BOUNDARY_VERIFIED'

  const midVoid = await verifySpatialVoidState(spatialRoot, undefined, engineRoot)
  const mutatedActiveVerified =
    !midVoid.isVoidState && midVoid.residualDiskArtifacts === 3
  if (!mutatedActiveVerified) {
    errors.push('Synthetic spatial mutation failed to populate disk artifacts')
  }

  // Step 3: Execute Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset
  await executeTabulaRasaSpatial(
    spatialModel,
    {
      idx: 'round-trip-spatial-reset',
      src: spatialRoot,
      dat: {
        repoRoot: spatialRoot,
        engineRoot,
        confirmToken1: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
        confirmToken2: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
        confirmToken3: SPATIAL_TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
      },
    },
    localBus,
  )

  // Step 4: Verify bit-for-bit Void_0 parity
  const postVoid = await verifySpatialVoidState(
    spatialRoot,
    localBus,
    engineRoot,
  )
  const postResetVoidVerified = postVoid.isVoidState
  if (!postResetVoidVerified) {
    errors.push('Post-reset spatial verification did not return Void State')
  }

  const zeroOrphanFilesVerified = postVoid.residualDiskArtifacts === 0
  if (!zeroOrphanFilesVerified) {
    errors.push(
      `Expected 0 residual disk artifacts, found ${postVoid.residualDiskArtifacts}`,
    )
  }

  const zeroModelContaminationVerified =
    spatialModel.totalSynchronousInvocations === 0 &&
    dbModel.loadedManifestCount === 0 &&
    dbModel.activeRootHash === null &&
    topologyModel.totalLoadedCells === 0 &&
    topologyModel.lastCompiledRootHash === GENESIS_HASH_64 &&
    ledgerModel.headSequence === 0 &&
    ledgerModel.lastEventHash === GENESIS_HASH_64 &&
    ledgerModel.entries.length === 0 &&
    occupancyModel.occupiedCells.size === 0 &&
    occupancyModel.totalTrackedActors === 0 &&
    edgeModel.partitionDampingOverrides.size === 0 &&
    capacityModel.cellCapacityOverrides.size === 0 &&
    overlayModel.closedPortalIds.size === 0 &&
    (globalThis as any).ACTIVE_SPATIAL_CLOCK_SNAPSHOT === undefined &&
    incidentModel.incidentReceiptCount === 0 &&
    witnessModel.stagedAppointmentProofs.length === 0 &&
    receiptModel.mintedReceipts.size === 0 &&
    portModel.totalQueriesProcessed === 0 &&
    collectModel.totalBatchesProcessed === 0 &&
    menuModel.statusMessage === 'SPATIAL_VOID_STATE_READY'

  if (!zeroModelContaminationVerified) {
    errors.push('In-memory spatial unit contamination survived Tabula Rasa Reset')
  }

  const valid = errors.length === 0
  const receipts: string[] = [
    `>> [SENTINEL:PARITY] Spatial round-trip Void-State parity certified [${valid ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}]`,
  ]
  await emitSentinelTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  return {
    valid,
    sandboxRoot: resolvedSandbox,
    initialVoidVerified,
    mutatedActiveVerified,
    postResetVoidVerified,
    zeroOrphanFilesVerified,
    zeroModelContaminationVerified,
    errors,
    receipts,
  }
}
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

**User Story** It was 11:03 PM on the production floor of `000.goblin-spatial`, three weeks after the Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset architecture had been commissioned. A newly provisioned Coding Agent had just opened a pull request adding a brand-new reactive domain unit (`52.gravity.unit`) to `packages/301.spatial/BEE.ts` and writing runtime geometry caches into an unregistered directory—forgetting to register `52.gravity.unit` in `SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST.unitRegistry` or wire a `RESET_GRAVITY` action and reducer case. Under the old paradigm, `52.gravity.unit` would have silently leaked stale gravitational vector state across resets and corrupted the next *Gotham Trinity Crisis* rooftop simulation. Instead, because **Phase 6: Unit Reset Coverage Sentinel In-Memory Verification** is encapsulated directly as a pure BEE unit buzzer inside `packages/301.spatial/03.laws.unit` (`buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.ts`) and mounted onto the Blessed Terminal Switchboard in `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit` (`[R] AUDIT SPATIAL TABULA RASA RESET COVERAGE`), the moment the Showrunner or CI runner triggers the audit at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$, `auditSpatialResetCoverage` parses `BEE.ts` against `SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST`, traps `52.gravity.unit` as an unhooked breach (`valid: false`, `val: -1`), and streams a crisp 7-bit ASCII alert into console pane `cns00` before a single downstream receipt is exported. Simultaneously, `verifySpatialRoundTripVoidParity` proves inside an isolated sandbox that mutating disk spools and all 16 stateful spatial units and executing the Triple-Lock reset returns `000.goblin-spatial` bit-for-bit back to **Posture A: Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State ($T_0 = \$0.00$)**.

**Strategic Goal** Supports the core architectural goal of **Self-Enforcing Architectural Governance & Zero-Drift Void-State Reversibility at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$**, guaranteeing that `000.goblin-spatial` automatically traps any newly added stateful BEE unit lacking `RESET_<UNIT>` wiring and continuously certifies bit-for-bit round-trip parity between pre-mutation Void State and post-reset Void State without standalone scripts under `scripts/`.

**Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Replaces standalone shell or Node scripts with pure, unit-encapsulated BEE audit buzzers (`auditSpatialResetCoverage` and `verifySpatialRoundTripVoidParity` in `packages/301.spatial/03.laws.unit/buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.ts`) wired directly into the Blessed Terminal Interface (`98.menu.unit`), giving both human Showrunners and autonomous PTY Coding Agents a single menu trigger (`[R] AUDIT SPATIAL TABULA RASA RESET COVERAGE`) to certify reset completeness and round-trip Void-State parity at $T_0 = \$0.00$.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Parsing `packages/301.spatial/BEE.ts` deterministically to verify 100% unit registration parity with `SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST.unitRegistry`, inspecting `<name>.action.ts` and `<name>.reduce.ts` on disk for every `STATEFUL_RESETTABLE` unit, executing a full 4-step synthetic mutation and Triple-Lock reset round-trip (`Void_0 -> Mutated Spatial State -> Triple-Lock Reset -> Void_0 Parity`) across all 16 stateful units and disk spools inside an isolated `os.tmpdir()` sandbox without touching live workspace files, and streaming pure 7-bit ASCII (`charCode <= 127`) telemetry to `cns00` with zero `console.log` calls.

**Interface**

- **`000.goblin-spatial` (`packages/301.spatial/03.laws.unit`):** Exports `AUDIT_SPATIAL_RESET_COVERAGE`, `AuditSpatialResetCoverage`, `AUDIT_SPATIAL_VOID_PARITY`, and `AuditSpatialVoidParity` in `laws.action.ts`; routes them via `clone-deep` in `laws.reduce.ts`; and exports `resolveSpatialPackageDirectory`, `auditSpatialResetCoverage`, `verifySpatialRoundTripVoidParity`, and `auditSpatialResetCoverageBuzz` in `buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.ts` and `laws.buzzer.ts`.
- **Spatial Core & Stateful Units (`38.types.unit`, `00.spatial.unit`, `05.clock.unit`, `07.database.unit`, `11.events.unit`, `12.topology.unit`, `14.port.unit`, `15.overlay.unit`, `19.ledger.unit`, `23.incident.unit`, `40.witness.unit`, `42.receipt.unit`, `44.occupancy.unit`, `49.edge.unit`, `51.capacity.unit`, `97.collect.unit`, `98.menu.unit`):** Consumes `SPATIAL_RESET_MANIFEST` and `validateSpatialResetManifest` from `38.types.unit`, triggers `executeTabulaRasaSpatial` and `verifySpatialVoidState` in `00.spatial.unit`, and verifies full reset parity across all 16 stateful spatial units and `GENESIS_HASH_64`.
